# Lossless archival Cargo manifests

Status: draft; fresh different-vendor PLAN approval required before edits to
existing artifacts. Serves Kadek foundation row 4 through Radio; discovered from
PR40's committed Git consumer. Parent: `ce0fb312faa8770e80efe9afa864106ae6b51552`.

## Result

Rename exactly seven archived harness manifests from `Cargo.toml` to
`Cargo.toml.txt`, preserving every byte. Keep source/workspace manifests and all
dependency pins, locks, Rust sources, tests and specifications unchanged. Cargo
must discover only the actual package when a fresh consumer pins an independent
committed candidate snapshot. The candidate is a local packaging witness, never
an origin release input.

Preserve the three original replay scripts under `verify.original.nu.txt` or
`reproduce.original.nu.txt` beside their existing names. Replace only those entry
scripts with thin Nu wrappers. Add one small shared staging helper in this task's
new audit directory. Preserve each CLI and its defaults explicitly:

| Entry | Default resolution before staging | Forwarded unchanged |
|---|---|---|
| Research `verify.nu` | `--repo`: original capsule's grandparent; `--siblings`: resolved repo's parent | Explicit resolved repo/siblings and optional `--live` |
| Execution `verify.nu` | `--repo`: original capsule's grandparent | Explicit resolved repo and optional `--live` |
| Slice `reproduce.nu` | `--radio-repo ~/cyber/radio`, `--hemera-repo ~/cyber/hemera`; retain these canonical defaults even from another worktree | Implementation revision, expanded selected repo paths, `--fetch`, `--full-workspace` |

Preserve original `--live` checks, including their historical HEAD equality. They
must still fail against a later merged HEAD. Research additionally checks a clean
tracked diff; execution does not. Do not add or infer another cleanliness check.

The helper copies only its selected capsule to a newly created disposable `/tmp`
directory, restores the explicitly listed Cargo logical names, and restores the
original replay script at its historical filename by moving the copied `.txt`
files, so the staged layout does not retain duplicate archived names. Replacing
the copied entry wrapper is the sole intentional collision, after checking its
identity; an existing logical Cargo path or missing archive fails before replay.
It must never create a Cargo.toml inside the real repository.
The original script then executes unchanged. All historical `files.json`, source
indices, contexts, reviews, fixture bytes, raw manifests and command receipts
remain unchanged. The wrapper is new packaging code, not the historically reviewed
verifier. Its own bytes and the original-to-archive mappings are bound by the new
capsule's manifest. `historical-index-bindings.json` records every affected index
entry: index path and JSON pointer, historical logical path/hash/length, satisfying
archived path, and deliberate direct-worktree outcome (missing logical Cargo path
or wrapper replacing original script). An already historical pre-CODE verifier
row is labeled separately and points to its existing preserved original. Physical
indices are deliberately historical; a direct unstaged check is not the replay.
Successful disposable replays are cleaned; failed ones retain
their isolated directory and propagate the original nonzero status.

   This makes the source path constant at the child's parse time and exposes the

4. Freeze an independent committed temporary candidate snapshot containing the

## Comparison boundary

The historical snapshots are labeled `historical-ce0-reader-before-runs.json` and
`historical-ce0-reader-after-runs.json`; their original timestamps/bytes remain
unchanged, and v1's frozen packet stays intact. They span the old reader runs,
not a repair. New paired measurements have separate ce0/candidate arm labels.

Within the new pair the local Radio Git URL is identical. Normalize only its
validated ce0/candidate revision tokens in package/source IDs, the two consumer
IDs and their exact consumer directories, the admitted checkout-root prefixes
in manifest/target paths, and recorded target/build/workspace directories. Every
replacement has an explicit old/new mapping and path-boundary check; no blanket
regex stripping. Resolve-node IDs/edges use those same exact ID substitutions.
Everything else must match, including features, dependency graph, package versions,
rust-version, target lists and dependency requirements. Historical HTTPS and new
local file URLs are intentionally different and are not a metadata equality claim.
Snapshot/receipt timestamps and Radio commit identities are retained as distinct
measurements, never edited to force equality. Consumer manifests/locks contain
their explicit arm pins; only production source/manifests/locks and reader stdout
have cross-arm byte-equality requirements.

## File scope

Seven manifest renames, each adding only `.txt`:

```text
audit/2026-10-09-kadek-slice-traversal/Cargo.toml
audit/2026-10-12-bao-proof-research/probe/Cargo.toml
```

Three entry wrappers replace their names while preserving exact original bytes:
`audit/2026-10-09-kadek-slice-traversal/reproduce.nu`,
`audit/2026-10-12-bao-proof-research/verify.nu`, and
`audit/2026-10-12-bao-bounded-proof/verify.nu`.
New helper, mappings, documentation, review inputs and raw evidence live only in
`audit/2026-10-13-audit-cargo-packaging/`; task artifacts live only in
`.claude/tasks/audit-cargo-packaging/`. No nested historical audit archive is copied.

## Five likely failures

1. A forgotten archived Cargo manifest still participates in discovery: exhaustive
   tracked basename inventory and a fresh committed-snapshot Git consumer.
2. Renaming silently changes historical bytes or hashes: compare all seven blobs,
   keep every historical index/receipt unchanged, replay the original scripts.
3. A wrapper reads temporary paths as the real source repository: resolve/pass
   explicit repo and sibling paths, including original CLI defaults.
4. Staging overwrites evidence or leaves an executable manifest in the repository:
   unique external destination, explicit mappings/collision checks, failure probe
   in a disposable copy, and before/after repository identity verification.
5. Packaging masks production/dependency drift: exact tree comparison, Git-pinned
   consumer identity, two unchanged literal result streams and honest warning counts.

Radio's cached registry `part_of = soft3` and Kadek's direct dependency remain
explicit. This is a packaging repair with no API/version floor change. There is
no release, tag, registry publication or Kadek dependency update in this unit.
The caller owns subsequent adoption of the actual merged Radio revision.
Previous broad workspace/rung and last-tag gate results remain unrefreshed; no
broader warning-free compiler, source, product or release claim follows.

between and after the runs. See `pair/` for exact revisions, argv and raw streams.

Replay (requires the original Radio Git objects, including ce0; Nu and Git):

The verifier checks retained hashes, exact historical Git inputs, the incremental

registry publication or Kadek dependency update occurs in this unit.

use ../2026-10-13-audit-cargo-packaging/replay.nu [stage finish]

def main [
  --implementation-revision: string
  --radio-repo: path = "~/cyber/radio"
  --hemera-repo: path = "~/cyber/hemera"
  --fetch
  --full-workspace
] {
  if $implementation_revision == null {
    error make {msg: '--implementation-revision must name the actual committed implementation'}
  }
  let staged = (stage $env.FILE_PWD reproduce.nu [Cargo.toml])
  mut args = [--implementation-revision $implementation_revision --radio-repo ($radio_repo | path expand) --hemera-repo ($hemera_repo | path expand)]
  if $fetch { $args = ($args | append '--fetch') }
  if $full_workspace { $args = ($args | append '--full-workspace') }
  let result = (^($nu.current-exe) ($staged.capsule | path join reproduce.nu) ...$args | complete)
  print --no-newline $result.stdout
  print --stderr --no-newline $result.stderr

===== 016 BEGIN audit/2026-10-09-kadek-slice-traversal/reproduce.original.nu.txt | 11037 bytes | SHA256 2429703bbea959fa84bbb6ee650f377bb8c93163009941fbddbfa055ca5a5e50 =====
# Replays immutable source inputs into fresh temporary workspaces; no repo writes.
def checked [action: closure] {
    let r = (do $action | complete)
    if $r.exit_code != 0 { error make {msg: $"exit ($r.exit_code)\n($r.stderr)\n($r.stdout)"} }
    $r
}

def record [run: path, name: string, command: string, action: closure] {
    let started = (date now)
    let r = (do $action | complete)
    let stem = ($run | path join logs $name)
    $r.stdout | save $"($stem).stdout"
    $r.stderr | save $"($stem).stderr"
    {command: $command, cwd: $env.PWD, started: $started, ended: (date now), exit: $r.exit_code}
        | to json | save $"($stem).json"
    print $"($name): exit ($r.exit_code)"
    $r
}

def prepare-crate [repo: path, revision: string, dir: path, receipt: path] {
    mkdir $dir
    let archive = $"($dir).tar"
    checked { ^git -C $repo archive --format=tar --output $archive $revision cyber-bao } | ignore
    checked { ^tar -xf $archive -C $dir } | ignore
    cp ($receipt | path join Cargo.toml) ($dir | path join Cargo.toml)
    cp ($receipt | path join Cargo.lock) ($dir | path join Cargo.lock)
    mkdir ($dir | path join src)
    cp ($receipt | path join probe.rs) ($dir | path join src main.rs)
    let manifest = ($dir | path join cyber-bao Cargo.toml)
    let original = (open --raw $manifest)
    if not ($original | str contains 'path = "../../hemera/rs"') {
        error make {msg: 'unexpected cyber-bao Hemera declaration'}
    }
    $original | str replace 'path = "../../hemera/rs"' 'version = "=0.3.1"' | save --force $manifest
}

def main [
    --implementation-revision: string # Required: actual committed source revision.
    --radio-repo: path = "~/cyber/radio"
    --hemera-repo: path = "~/cyber/hemera"
    --fetch # Fetch pinned dependencies when caches are incomplete; never regenerate locks.
    --full-workspace # Also export exact-source Hemera and run the broader native gates.
] {
    if $implementation_revision == null {
        error make {msg: '--implementation-revision must name the actual committed implementation'}
    }
    let receipt = $env.FILE_PWD
    let repo = ($radio_repo | path expand)
    let hemera = ($hemera_repo | path expand)
    let base = 'db1d62e2cd1e4b2f309fa19bcc158bd29b44753d'
    let hemera_rev = '23f3bbcff910ea6d504ceb505680a539260869da'
    let implementation = ((checked { ^git -C $repo rev-parse $"($implementation_revision)^{commit}" }).stdout | str trim)
    let run = ((checked { ^mktemp -d /tmp/radio-slice-replay.XXXXXX }).stdout | str trim)
    mkdir ($run | path join logs)
    print $"logs and exported inputs: ($run)"
    checked { ^git -C $repo merge-base --is-ancestor $base $implementation } | ignore
    {radio_base: $base, implementation: $implementation, hemera_source: $hemera_rev,
        hemera_registry: '=0.3.1', checksum: 'ebcf7ffcd69170adde7e792826d7cececda962ea220b9834c6535bbc71e5a6df'}
        | to json | save ($run | path join sources.json)
    (checked { ^rustc -Vv }).stdout | save ($run | path join rustc.txt)
    (checked { ^cargo -Vv }).stdout | save ($run | path join cargo.txt)
    let baseline = ($run | path join baseline)
    let candidate = ($run | path join candidate)
    prepare-crate $repo $base $baseline $receipt
    prepare-crate $repo $implementation $candidate $receipt
    for source in (open ($receipt | path join SOURCE-FREEZE.json)) {
        let actual = ((checked { ^git hash-object ($candidate | path join $source.path) }).stdout | str trim)
        if $actual != $source.blob { error make {msg: $"reviewed source mismatch: ($source.path)"} }
    }
    let original = ((checked { ^git -C $repo ls-tree -r $base cyber-bao }).stdout
        | lines | parse -r '^(?P<mode>\d+) (?P<type>\w+) (?P<blob>[0-9a-f]+)\t(?P<path>.+)$'
        | where {|p| $p.path | str ends-with '.rs' })
    if ($original | length) != 15 { error make {msg: 'unexpected original Rust inventory'} }
    for source in $original {
        let actual = ((checked { ^git hash-object ($baseline | path join $source.path) }).stdout | str trim)
        if $actual != $source.blob { error make {msg: $"baseline source mismatch: ($source.path)"} }
    }
    cd $baseline
    if $fetch { checked { ^cargo fetch --locked } | ignore }
    let probe = (with-env {RUSTFLAGS: '', RUSTDOCFLAGS: ''} {
        record $run historical-probe 'cargo run --offline --locked --bin kadek-bao-source-repro' {
            ^cargo run --offline --locked --bin kadek-bao-source-repro
        }
    })
    if $probe.exit_code != 0 { error make {msg: 'historical probe failed; see logs'} }
    checked { ^diff -u ($receipt | path join expected-probe.stdout) ($run | path join logs historical-probe.stdout) } | ignore
    (checked { ^shasum -a 256 -c ($receipt | path join FIXTURE-SHA256SUMS) }).stdout | save ($run | path join fixture-check.txt)
    with-env {RUSTFLAGS: '-Dwarnings'} {
        record $run pristine-clippy 'RUSTFLAGS=-Dwarnings cargo clippy -p cyber-bao --all-features --all-targets --offline --locked -- -D warnings' {
            ^cargo clippy -p cyber-bao --all-features --all-targets --offline --locked -- -D warnings
        } | ignore
    }
    # Copy only the independently constructed new regression onto unchanged production.
    mkdir ($baseline | path join cyber-bao tests)
    cp ($candidate | path join cyber-bao tests slice_traversal.rs) ($baseline | path join cyber-bao tests slice_traversal.rs)
    for profile in [debug release] {
        let args = if $profile == 'release' { ['--release'] } else { [] }
        let r = (with-env {RUSTFLAGS: '', RUSTDOCFLAGS: ''} {
            record $run $"red-($profile)" $"cargo test -p cyber-bao --test slice_traversal literal_right_subtree_is_authenticated ($args | str join ' ') --offline --locked" {
                ^cargo test -p cyber-bao --test slice_traversal literal_right_subtree_is_authenticated ...$args --offline --locked
            }
        })
        if $r.exit_code != 101 or not ($r.stdout | str contains 'independently constructed valid proof must decode: Truncated') {
            error make {msg: $"red-($profile) did not reproduce the expected assertion; see logs"}
        }
    }
    cd $candidate
    if $fetch { checked { ^cargo fetch --locked } | ignore }
    with-env {RUSTFLAGS: '-Dwarnings', RUSTDOCFLAGS: '-Dwarnings'} {
        for args in ([
            [test -p cyber-bao --offline --locked],
            [test -p cyber-bao --all-features --offline --locked],
            [test -p cyber-bao --all-features --release --offline --locked],
            [check -p cyber-bao --all-targets --no-default-features --offline --locked],
            [clippy -p cyber-bao --all-targets --offline --locked -- -D warnings],
            [clippy -p cyber-bao --all-targets --no-default-features --offline --locked -- -D warnings],
            [clippy -p cyber-bao --all-targets --all-features --offline --locked -- -D warnings],
            [doc -p cyber-bao --all-features --no-deps --offline --locked],
            [semver-checks check-release -p cyber-bao --baseline-root ../baseline],
        ] | enumerate) {
            record $run $"candidate-($args.index)" $"RUSTFLAGS=-Dwarnings RUSTDOCFLAGS=-Dwarnings cargo ($args.item | str join ' ')" {
                ^cargo ...$args.item
            } | ignore
        }
        record $run msrv 'RUSTFLAGS=-Dwarnings rustup run 1.89.0 cargo check -p cyber-bao --all-features --all-targets --offline --locked' {
            ^rustup run 1.89.0 cargo check -p cyber-bao --all-features --all-targets --offline --locked
        } | ignore
    }
    if $full_workspace {
        let full = ($run | path join full)
        mkdir ($full | path join radio) ($full | path join hemera)
        let top = ((checked { ^git -C $repo ls-tree --name-only $implementation }).stdout | lines | where {|p| $p != 'nettools' })
        let net = ((checked { ^git -C $repo ls-tree --name-only $"($implementation):nettools" }).stdout | lines | where {|p| $p != 'target' } | each {|p| $"nettools/($p)" })
        checked { ^git -C $repo archive --format=tar --output ($run | path join radio-full.tar) $implementation ...($top | append $net) } | ignore
        checked { ^tar -xf ($run | path join radio-full.tar) -C ($full | path join radio) } | ignore
        checked { ^git -C $hemera archive --format=tar --output ($run | path join hemera.tar) $hemera_rev } | ignore
        checked { ^tar -xf ($run | path join hemera.tar) -C ($full | path join hemera) } | ignore
        cd ($full | path join radio)
        if $fetch { checked { ^cargo fetch --locked } | ignore }
        let metadata = (record $run full-metadata 'cargo metadata --format-version 1 --all-features --offline --locked' {
            ^cargo metadata --format-version 1 --all-features --offline --locked
        })
        if $metadata.exit_code != 0 { error make {msg: 'full metadata prerequisite failed; see logs'} }
        let local = ($metadata.stdout | from json | get packages | where source == null | select name version manifest_path)
        let isolated = ($full | path expand)
        if ($local | any {|p| not ($p.manifest_path | str starts-with $isolated) }) {
            error make {msg: 'local dependency escapes isolated source root'}
        }
        $local | to json | save ($run | path join full-local-packages.json)
        with-env {RUSTFLAGS: '-Dwarnings', RUSTDOCFLAGS: '-Dwarnings'} {
            for args in ([
                [test -p cyber-bao --all-features --offline --locked],
                [check --workspace --all-features --all-targets --offline --locked],
                [test --workspace --lib --bins --tests --offline --locked],
                [make format-check], [nextest run --workspace --lib --bins --tests --offline --locked],
                [deny -D warnings check],
            ] | enumerate) {
                record $run $"full-($args.index)" $"RUSTFLAGS=-Dwarnings RUSTDOCFLAGS=-Dwarnings cargo ($args.item | str join ' ')" { ^cargo ...$args.item } | ignore
            }
        }
        record $run format-expanded 'rustup run nightly-2025-11-26 cargo fmt --all --check -- --config unstable_features=true --config imports_granularity=Crate,group_imports=StdExternalCrate,reorder_imports=true,format_code_in_doc_comments=true' {
            ^rustup run nightly-2025-11-26 cargo fmt --all --check -- --config unstable_features=true --config imports_granularity=Crate,group_imports=StdExternalCrate,reorder_imports=true,format_code_in_doc_comments=true
        } | ignore
    }
    let outcomes = (glob ($run | path join logs '*.json') | each {|p| open $p | insert name ($p | path parse | get stem) })
    $outcomes | to json | save ($run | path join outcomes.json)
    let failed = ($outcomes | where {|r| $r.exit != 0 and not ($r.name | str starts-with 'red-') and $r.name != 'pristine-clippy' })
    print ($outcomes | select name exit command)
    print $"Evidence retained at ($run). Expected red tests are not product passes; no upstream/row-4 closure follows."
    if not ($failed | is-empty) { exit 1 }
}

def main [--repo: string, --siblings: string, --live] {
  let own = if $repo == null { $env.FILE_PWD | path dirname | path dirname } else { $repo | path expand }
  let parent = if $siblings == null { $own | path dirname } else { $siblings | path expand }

===== 018 BEGIN audit/2026-10-12-bao-proof-research/verify.original.nu.txt | 4241 bytes | SHA256 70ccf93dd5c30e9fd1eea057190655f886b6919b1365fb481f60e8bc433e6f78 =====
use std/assert

def check-file [root: string, row: record] {
  let path = ($root | path join $row.path)
  assert equal (open --raw $path | hash sha256) $row.sha256
  assert equal (ls $path | get size | first | into int) $row.bytes
}

def git-bytes [repo: string, revision: string, path: string, temp: string] {
  ^git -C $repo cat-file blob $'($revision):($path)' out> $temp
  assert equal $env.LAST_EXIT_CODE 0
  open --raw $temp | into binary
}

def main [--repo: string, --siblings: string, --live] {
  let root = $env.FILE_PWD
  let own = if $repo == null { $root | path dirname | path dirname } else { $repo }
  let parent = if $siblings == null { $own | path dirname } else { $siblings }
  let repos = {radio: $own, hemera: ($parent | path join hemera),
    kadek: ($parent | path join kadek), soft3: ($parent | path join soft3)}
  let files = (open ($root | path join files.json))
  for f in $files { check-file $root $f }
  let initial = (open ($root | path join inputs.json))
  for f in $initial.probe_inputs { check-file $root $f }
  for f in (open ($root | path join primary-inputs.json) | get files) { check-file $root $f }
  let support = (open ($root | path join support-inputs.json))
  let bridge = (open ($root | path join merged-bridge.json))
  let temp = (^mktemp /tmp/radio-proof-replay.XXXXXX | str trim)
  let git_inputs = ($initial.git_inputs | append $support | append $bridge.merged_inputs)
  for f in $git_inputs {
    let bytes = (git-bytes ($repos | get $f.repository) $f.revision $f.path $temp)
    assert equal ($bytes | hash sha256) $f.sha256
    assert equal ($bytes | bytes length) $f.bytes
    if $f.local? != null { assert equal (open --raw ($root | path join $f.local) | into binary) $bytes }
  }
  for f in $bridge.comparison {
    let old = (git-bytes $own $bridge.parent $f.path $temp)
    let new = (git-bytes $own $bridge.current $f.path $temp)
    assert equal ($old | hash sha256) $f.parent_sha256
    assert equal ($new | hash sha256) $f.sha256
    assert equal (($old | hash sha256) == ($new | hash sha256)) $f.unchanged
  }
  assert equal (open --raw ($root | path join prerequisite/traversal.rs)) (open --raw ($root | path join merged-prerequisite/traversal.rs))
  for e in (open ($root | path join excerpts.json)) {
    let bytes = (git-bytes ($repos | get $e.repository) $e.revision $e.source $temp)
    assert equal ($bytes | hash sha256) $e.source_sha256
    let lines = ($bytes | decode utf-8 | split row "\n")
    let text = (($lines | skip ($e.first - 1) | take ($e.last - $e.first + 1) | str join "\n") + "\n")
    assert equal ($text | hash sha256) $e.sha256
  }
  let runner = (open --raw ($root | path join records/capture.rb) | hash sha256)
  let raw = (glob ($root | path join 'records/*.json'))
  for path in $raw {
    let record = (open $path)
    assert equal $record.runner_sha256 $runner
    let base = ($path | str replace --regex '\.json$' '')
    for stream in [stdout stderr] {
      let body = (open --raw $'($base).($stream)' | into binary)
      assert equal ($body | hash sha256) ($record | get $stream | get sha256)
      assert equal ($body | bytes length) ($record | get $stream | get bytes)
    }
  }
  assert equal (open --raw ($root | path join records/initial-debug.stdout)) (open --raw ($root | path join records/legacy-release.stdout))
  assert equal (open --raw ($root | path join records/primary-debug.stdout)) (open --raw ($root | path join records/primary-release.stdout))
  let fixtures = (open ($root | path join fixtures.json))
  for f in $fixtures { check-file $root $f }
  for f in (open ($root | path join task-inputs.json)) {
    check-file $root {path: $f.local, sha256: $f.sha256, bytes: $f.bytes}
    if $live { check-file $own $f }
  }
  if $live {
    assert equal (^git -C $own rev-parse HEAD | str trim) $bridge.current
    assert equal (^git -C $own diff --name-only | str trim) ''
  }
  rm $temp
  {status: pass, files: ($files | length), git_input_records: ($git_inputs | length),
    bridge_comparisons: ($bridge.comparison | length), primary_excerpts: 13,
    raw_receipts: ($raw | length), fixtures: ($fixtures | length),
    production_implemented: false, cargo_rerun: false, historical_inventory_archive_replayed: false} | to json
}

use ../2026-10-13-audit-cargo-packaging/replay.nu [stage finish]

def main [--repo: string, --live] {
  let own = if $repo == null { $env.FILE_PWD | path dirname | path dirname } else { $repo | path expand }
  let staged = (stage $env.FILE_PWD verify.nu [fixture-probe/Cargo.toml nostd/Cargo.toml nostd-parent/Cargo.toml seals/Cargo.toml])
  let args = ([--repo $own] | append (if $live { [--live] } else { [] }))
  let result = (^($nu.current-exe) ($staged.capsule | path join verify.nu) ...$args | complete)
  print --no-newline $result.stdout
  print --stderr --no-newline $result.stderr
  finish $staged $result.exit_code
}

===== 020 BEGIN audit/2026-10-12-bao-bounded-proof/verify.original.nu.txt | 10975 bytes | SHA256 dd3c97295aac25f832c4b39c1a8f654e478212cefa790ecd29e6285ac63ae63a =====
use std/assert

def check [root: string, path: string, bytes: int, digest: string] {
  let data = (open --raw ($root | path join $path) | into binary)
  assert equal ($data | bytes length) $bytes
  assert equal ($data | hash sha256) $digest
}

def main [--repo: string, --live] {
  let root = $env.FILE_PWD
  let own = if $repo == null { $root | path dirname | path dirname } else { $repo }
  let all = (open ($root | path join files.json))
  for f in $all { check $root $f.path $f.bytes $f.sha256 }
  let ready = (open ($root | path join source-complete.json))
  let freezes = [tests-first.json source-freeze.json source-final.json source-ready.json source-complete.json]
  mut candidates = []
  for p in $freezes {
    let freeze = (open ($root | path join $p))
    for f in $freeze.files {
      check $root $f.local $f.bytes $f.sha256
      $candidates = ($candidates | append $f)
    }
  }
  for f in (open ($root | path join fixtures-first.json) | get files) {
    check $root $f.path $f.bytes $f.sha256
  }
  for p in [seals-first.json seals-final-inputs.json] {
    for f in (open ($root | path join $p)) { check $root $f.path $f.bytes $f.sha256 }
  }
  let temp = (^mktemp /tmp/radio-proof-execution-replay.XXXXXX | str trim)
  mut original_count = 0
  mut candidate_count = 0
  let epochs = (glob ($root | path join 'epochs/*.json'))
  for p in $epochs {
    let epoch = (open $p)
    assert equal $epoch.head $ready.parent
    assert equal ($epoch.files | to json --raw | hash sha256) ($p | path parse | get stem)
    for row in $epoch.files {
      let path = ($row | get 0)
      let size = ($row | get 1)
      let digest = ($row | get 2)
      let local = ($candidates | where {|f| $f.path == $path and $f.sha256 == $digest })
      if ($local | is-not-empty) {
        assert equal ($local | first | get bytes) $size
        $candidate_count += 1
      } else {
        ^git -C $own cat-file blob $'($epoch.head):($path)' out> $temp
        assert equal $env.LAST_EXIT_CODE 0
        let data = (open --raw $temp | into binary)
        assert equal ($data | bytes length) $size
        assert equal ($data | hash sha256) $digest
        $original_count += 1
      }
    }
  }
  let runner = (open --raw ($root | path join records/capture.rb) | hash sha256)
  let raw = (glob ($root | path join 'records/*.json'))
  for p in $raw {
    let r = (open $p)
    assert equal $r.runner_sha256 $runner
    let stem = ($p | str replace --regex '\.json$' '')
    for stream in [stdout stderr] {
      let data = (open --raw $'($stem).($stream)' | into binary)
      assert equal ($data | bytes length) ($r | get $stream | get bytes)
      assert equal ($data | hash sha256) ($r | get $stream | get sha256)
    }
  }
  let bindings = (glob ($root | path join 'bindings/*.json'))
  let gate_runners = ([records/final-gates.rb records/radio-bao-proof-final-gates-v2.rb] | each {|p| open --raw ($root | path join $p) | hash sha256 })
  for p in $bindings {
    let b = (open $p)
    assert equal $b.source_before $b.source_after
    assert ($b.runner_sha256 in $gate_runners)
    assert equal (open --raw ($root | path join records $'($b.name).json') | hash sha256) $b.record_sha256
    assert ( ($root | path join epochs $'($b.source_before).json') | path exists )
  }
  let ledger = (open ($root | path join gate-ledger.json))
  for row in $ledger {
    let record = (open ($root | path join $row.record))
    assert equal (open --raw ($root | path join $row.record) | hash sha256) $row.record_sha256
    assert equal $record.exit_code $row.exit
    assert equal $record.argv $row.argv
    assert equal $record.revision $row.revision
    assert equal $record.stdout $row.stdout
    assert equal $record.stderr $row.stderr
    if $row.epoch != null {
      assert equal (open ($root | path join bindings $'($row.name).json') | get source_before) $row.epoch
    }
  }
  for row in (open ($root | path join review-gates.json)) {
    assert equal $row ($ledger | where name == $row.name | first)
  }
  let pairs = ((open ($root | path join comparisons.json)) | append (open ($root | path join additional-comparisons.json)))
  for pair in $pairs {
    assert ($pair.candidate in ($ledger | get name))
    assert ($pair.parent in ($ledger | get name))
  }
  let previous = (open ($root | path join source-ready.json))
  let changed = ($ready.files | where {|f| $f.sha256 != ($previous.files | where path == $f.path | first | get sha256) } | get path)
  assert equal $changed ['cyber-bao/tests/proof.rs']
  for row in (open ($root | path join seals-final-results.json)) {
    let record = (open ($root | path join records $'seal-($row.bin)-final.json'))
    assert equal $record.exit_code 101
    assert equal $row.actual [$row.expected]
    let stderr = (open --raw ($root | path join records $'seal-($row.bin)-final.stderr'))
    let codes = ($stderr | parse --regex 'error\[(E\d+)\]' | get capture0 | uniq)
    assert equal $codes [$row.expected]
  }
  assert equal (open ($root | path join records/seal-control-final.json) | get exit_code) 0
  for f in (open ($root | path join interleaved-oracle.json) | get files) {
    check $root $f.path $f.bytes $f.sha256
  }
  let review = (open ($root | path join plan-review/invocation.json))
  let completion = (open ($root | path join plan-review/completion.json))
  assert equal (open --raw ($root | path join plan-review/launcher.rb) | hash sha256) $review.launcher_sha256
  assert equal (open --raw ($root | path join plan-input/context.txt) | hash sha256) $review.input_sha256
  assert equal (open --raw ($root | path join plan-review/stdout.json) | hash sha256) $completion.stdout_sha256
  assert equal (open --raw ($root | path join plan-review/stderr.txt) | hash sha256) $completion.stderr_sha256
  assert equal (open ($root | path join plan-review/stdout.json) | get result) (open --raw ($root | path join plan-review/result.md))
  for pack in [plan-input code-input] {
    let manifest = (open ($root | path join $pack manifest.json))
    check $root ($pack | path join context.txt) $manifest.context_bytes $manifest.context_sha256
  }
  let final_review = (open ($root | path join review/code/invocation.json))
  let final_completion = (open ($root | path join review/code/completion.json))
  assert equal (open --raw ($root | path join review/code/launcher.rb) | hash sha256) $final_review.launcher_sha256
  assert equal (open --raw ($root | path join code-input/context.txt) | hash sha256) $final_review.input_sha256
  assert equal (open --raw ($root | path join review/code/stdout.json) | hash sha256) $final_completion.stdout_sha256
  assert equal (open --raw ($root | path join review/code/stderr.txt) | hash sha256) $final_completion.stderr_sha256
  assert equal $final_completion.exit_code 0
  assert equal (open ($root | path join review/code/stdout.json) | get result) (open --raw ($root | path join review/code/result.md))
  let analysis = (open ($root | path join review/code/analysis.json))
  for name in [owner-clippy-all-final parent-owner-clippy-all-final] {
    let text = (open --raw ($root | path join records $'($name).stderr'))
    let parsed = ($text | parse --regex '(?s)error: (?P<message>[^\n]+)\n\s+--> (?P<path>[^\n]+):(?P<line>\d+):(?P<column>\d+).*?index\.html#(?P<lint>[a-z_]+)' | each {|r| {message: $'error: ($r.message)', path: $r.path, line: ($r.line | into int), column: ($r.column | into int), lint: $r.lint} })
    let key = if $name == owner-clippy-all-final { 'candidate_diagnostics' } else { 'parent_diagnostics' }
    assert equal $parsed ($analysis.clippy | get $key)
  }
  assert equal ($analysis.clippy.candidate_diagnostics | first 11) $analysis.clippy.parent_diagnostics
  assert equal ($analysis.clippy.candidate_diagnostics | length) 14
  for f in $analysis.clippy.source_identities {
    ^git -C $own cat-file blob $'($f.parent):($f.path)' out> $temp
    assert equal $env.LAST_EXIT_CODE 0
    assert equal (open --raw $temp | hash sha256) $f.parent_sha256
    assert equal $f.candidate_sha256 $f.parent_sha256
    if $live { check $own $f.path $f.bytes $f.candidate_sha256 }
  }
  for pair in $analysis.formatting {
    let candidate = (open --raw ($root | path join records $'($pair.candidate).stdout'))
    let parent = (open --raw ($root | path join records $'($pair.parent).stdout'))
    assert equal ($candidate | hash sha256) $pair.candidate_sha256
    assert equal ($parent | hash sha256) $pair.parent_sha256
    let normalized_candidate = ($candidate | str replace --all '/Users/master/cyber/radio-bao-bounded-proof' '<REPO>' | str replace --all 'Diff in <REPO>/cyber-bao/src/lib.rs:22:' 'Diff in <REPO>/cyber-bao/src/lib.rs:21:')
    let normalized_parent = ($parent | str replace --all '/private/tmp/radio-bao-proof-parent-b933' '<REPO>')
    assert equal $normalized_candidate $normalized_parent
    assert equal ($normalized_candidate | hash sha256) $pair.normalized_sha256
    assert equal (open --raw ($root | path join records $'($pair.candidate).stderr')) (open --raw ($root | path join records $'($pair.parent).stderr'))
  }
  for name in [root-code root-capsule] {
    let receipt = (open ($root | path join review/code $'($name).json'))
    assert equal $receipt.exit_code 0
    assert equal $receipt.runner_sha256 $runner
    for stream in [stdout stderr] {
      let data = (open --raw ($root | path join review/code $'($name).($stream)') | into binary)
      assert equal ($data | bytes length) ($receipt | get $stream | get bytes)
      assert equal ($data | hash sha256) ($receipt | get $stream | get sha256)
    }
  }
  let first = (open ($root | path join records/parent-absence.json))
  assert equal $first.revision $ready.parent
  assert equal $first.exit_code 101
  assert (open --raw ($root | path join records/parent-absence.stderr) | str contains 'error[E0432]')
  for f in $ready.files {
    if $live { check $own $f.path $f.bytes $f.sha256 }
    if ($f.path | str starts-with 'cyber-bao/src/') {
      if $f.path == 'cyber-bao/src/lib.rs' {
        ^git -C $own cat-file blob $'($ready.parent):($f.path)' out> $temp
        assert equal $env.LAST_EXIT_CODE 0
        let old = (open --raw $temp)
        let new = (open --raw ($root | path join $f.local))
        assert equal ($new | str replace "pub mod proof;\n" '') $old
      }
    }
  }
  if $live { assert equal (^git -C $own rev-parse HEAD | str trim) $ready.parent }
  rm $temp
  {status: pass, files: ($all | length), source_epochs: ($epochs | length),
    original_git_source_records: $original_count, candidate_source_records: $candidate_count,
    raw_records: ($raw | length), gate_bindings: ($bindings | length),
    counterpart_mappings: ($pairs | length),
    initial_seed_fixtures: 17, supplemental_interleaved_carriers: 1,
    code_review_exit: $final_completion.exit_code,
    clippy_matched_diagnostics: 11, extra_candidate_diagnostics_source_attributed: 3,
    cargo_rerun: false, hemera_recomputed: false,
    requires_exact_parent_git_objects: true} | to json
}

===== 021 BEGIN audit/2026-10-13-audit-cargo-packaging/replay.nu | 1795 bytes | SHA256 a8f26e4ce221fa18ddd99eb622015e6327e8df2f480386c747ab163619f3b151 =====
use std/assert

# Historical names exist only in this disposable copy, never in the Git source.
export def stage [root: path, entry: string, manifests: list<string>] {
  assert ($entry in [verify.nu reproduce.nu]) 'Unexpected replay entry'
  let archived_entry = ($entry | str replace '.nu' '.original.nu.txt')
  let originals = ($manifests | append $entry)
  let archives = ($manifests | each {|p| $p + '.txt' } | append $archived_entry)
  for p in $manifests {
    assert (not (($root | path join $p) | path exists)) $'Logical Cargo path already exists: ($p)'
  }
  let hashes = ($archives | each {|p| open --raw ($root | path join $p) | hash sha256 })
  let wrapper = (open --raw ($root | path join $entry) | hash sha256)
  let directory = (^mktemp -d /tmp/radio-audit-replay.XXXXXX | str trim)
  assert equal $env.LAST_EXIT_CODE 0
  let capsule = ($directory | path join capsule)
  try {
    cp --recursive $root $capsule
    assert equal (open --raw ($capsule | path join $entry) | hash sha256) $wrapper
    for row in ($archives | enumerate) {
      let source = ($capsule | path join $row.item)
      assert equal (open --raw $source | hash sha256) ($hashes | get $row.index)
      let logical = ($originals | get $row.index)
      if $logical != $entry {
        assert (not (($capsule | path join $logical) | path exists)) 'Staging collision'
      }
      mv --force $source ($capsule | path join $logical)
    }
  } catch {|failure|
    error make {msg: $'($failure.msg); failed staging retained at ($directory)'}
  }
  {directory: $directory, capsule: $capsule}
}

export def finish [staged: record, status: int] {
  if $status == 0 {
    rm --recursive $staged.directory
  } else {
    print --stderr $'Failed historical replay retained at ($staged.directory)'
  }
  exit $status
}

# iroh_docsrs" cargo +nightly doc --all-features.  We use our own
# iroh_docsrs instead of the common docsrs to avoid also enabling this

===== 026 BEGIN audit/2026-10-13-audit-cargo-packaging/review/plan-v2/result.md | 8517 bytes | SHA256 9397ff948d59b5736c6fce0322913a71dd4aea3b7364934f9a30eeb767205544 =====
# Independent PLAN Review v2 — `audit-cargo-packaging`

## Verdict: APPROVE

I re-derived the causal chain from the supplied bodies rather than trusting the narrative. Item 006 (`nostd-parent/Cargo.toml`, line 7) declares `cyber-bao = { path = "/tmp/radio-bao-proof-parent-b933/cyber-bao", default-features = false }`. Cargo's Git source enumerates every manifest in a checkout and recursively resolves their path dependencies; that absolute path resolves outside the checkout to a second on-disk `cyber-bao v0.1.0`, producing exactly the text in `records/consumer-ce0-lock.stderr` (038) and `-metadata.stderr` (041). The other six manifests use `../../../cyber-bao`, which resolves to the checkout's own `cyber-bao/Cargo.toml` (016) and dedupes silently against the same package — so the defect has a single origin and renaming all seven is a conservative over-fix, not a shotgun. The diagnosis stands.

All six v1 blockers are closed, and closed with measurement rather than prose:

**B1 (vacuous warning check).** Step 5 now requires the external probe manifest's existence/length/hash before, between and after both arms, an explicit "absence invalidates the witness" clause on the ce0 arm, and one private object store serving both arms through the same `file://` URL with only the Radio revision differing. This is the correct control: the differential now fails loudly if `/tmp/radio-bao-proof-parent-b933` has been reaped, instead of silently reporting a repair it never demonstrated. The explicit refusal to delete or alter that directory also prevents the measurement from destroying its own witness.

**B2 (no feasible invocation mechanism).** This is now *measured*, not proposed. `prepare-crate` in item 011 is a plain `def`, so it is not importable, and Nu requires `source` paths to be parse-time constants. The resolution — a child `nu -c` whose code string embeds a quoted literal path, making it constant at the child's parse time while exposing the private definition — is exactly right, and item 054 shows the literal code with item 048 showing exit 0 without Cargo. Items 034 and 032 confirm the six exported manifest/lock/probe copies match their inputs byte-for-byte, with 16/17 complete `cyber-bao` exports and only the function's own existing `path = "../../hemera/rs"` → `version = "=0.3.1"` substitution. The plan correctly marks this as mechanism-only and explicitly declines to rerun the codec matrix (which would be the broad gate this unit does not own). All five surrounding `$receipt` inputs I flagged are now enumerated in step 3 and frozen in item 032.

**B3 (impossible metadata equality).** Narrowed correctly. Byte-equality is now confined to reader stdout and production source/manifests/locks; the metadata comparison is an explicit enumerated substitution list (validated ce0/candidate revision tokens, two consumer IDs, consumer directories, admitted checkout-root prefixes, target/build/workspace directories) with path-boundary checks and no blanket regex. The clause requiring resolve-node IDs/edges to use *those same exact* substitutions is important and present — item 040 shows package ids and `deps[].pkg` strings carrying the full rev, so an inconsistent substitution would have silently broken the graph comparison. The admission that historical HTTPS vs. new local `file://` URLs are intentionally different and are not an equality claim is the honest framing.

**B4 (indices inconsistent with tree).** `historical-index-bindings.json` (033) resolves this well. I checked its internal coherence: the seven rename rows' hashes/lengths match items 004–010 exactly; the two wrapper rows (`/88` in research `files.json`, `/618` in execution `files.json`) match items 012 and 013 and the selected index rows in 024/025; the repeated input indices (`inputs.json`, `primary-inputs.json`, `seals-first.json`, `seals-final-inputs.json`, two `plan-input/manifest.json` sections) are covered; and the pre-CODE verifier row is labeled distinctly as `already_historical_variant_before_packaging` pointing at `review/code/verify-before-code.nu` (7381 bytes, a different hash) rather than being conflated with the new wrapper. The `planned_direct_check` field makes each deliberate physical mismatch machine-readable. Original indices stay untouched, which is right given both 2026-10-12 `files.json` self-hash their own verifier.

**B5 (contradictory default resolution).** The per-script table in the Result section is now unambiguous and matches the code: both verifiers derive `$own` from `FILE_PWD` grandparent (items 012, 013) and must receive explicitly resolved paths, while the reproducer's `~/cyber/radio` / `~/cyber/hemera` defaults (item 011) are retained verbatim even from another worktree. The `--live` semantics are stated accurately: research checks HEAD equality *and* a clean tracked diff, execution checks HEAD only, and no new cleanliness assertion is invented. The explicit note that these must still fail against a later merged HEAD is the correct disclosure.

**B6 (mislabeled snapshots).** Renamed to `historical-ce0-reader-before-runs.json` / `-after-runs.json` (029, 030) with bytes and timestamps preserved; I confirmed both carry identical ce0 source hashes and identical `kadek_parent`, differing only in `at` (01:20:13 vs 01:23:51), so the new labels describe what they actually are. V1's frozen copies retain their original labels, and future arms get arm-specific names.

Scope discipline is accurate throughout: no Rust/API/spec/version change, no release or tag, no Kadek dependency update, soft3 `part_of` and Kadek's direct-consumer status stated, prior broad reds explicitly unrefreshed and not relabeled, and the warning-free claim bounded to the enumerated invocations. The staging design — copy only the selected capsule to a fresh `/tmp` directory, *move* the restored `.txt` files so no duplicate archived names remain, treat the entry-wrapper replacement as the sole intentional collision after an identity check, fail before replay on a pre-existing logical Cargo path or missing archive, and never create a `Cargo.toml` inside the real repository — is the right shape, and the failure probe plus before/after repository identity check give it teeth.

## Advice (non-blocking, do not re-review)

- Still worth adopting the generalized invariant rather than only the basename inventory: assert that no tracked manifest anywhere declares a path dependency that is absolute or escapes the repository root. That is the actual defect class; the seven renames are one instance. It also covers `quinn` and `nettools`, which are workspace-`exclude`d in item 015 but still enumerated by Cargo's Git source and were not supplied to me. Declining a global source-manifest *cleanup* for lack of evidence is defensible; adding a cheap *guard* is not the same expansion.
- Pair that with the audit-basename guard the response already proposes, so the next capsule commits `Cargo.toml.txt` from the outset instead of reintroducing this.
- In the failure probe, assert the real repository's `audit/` tree hash is unchanged after the deliberate failure, not just that no `Cargo.toml` appeared — a staging bug that writes *outside* the disposable directory is the expensive one.
- Record `nostd-parent/Cargo.toml.txt`'s absolute path as nonportable-but-reconstructable, as the response states, so no future agent "repairs" the frozen bytes. The response's refusal to call it permanently unreplayable is correct and better than v1's framing.
- When normalizing metadata, apply substitutions to the parsed JSON rather than the raw stdout, so a path-boundary check cannot accidentally match inside an unrelated string field. Item 040 contains both `target_directory` and many `src_path` values sharing the same prefix; structural substitution makes the old/new mapping auditable per field.
- Keep step 2's "complete committed tree comparison outside the allowed paths must be empty" literal and machine-checked, including root `Cargo.toml`, `cyber-bao/Cargo.toml` and root `Cargo.lock` as already specified. It remains the strongest single control here.

One standing limitation, already disclosed and not a blocker: the observed warning depends on a machine-local directory, so this witness is local and not universal. The plan says so, claims no isolated ring-0 radius, and bounds its verification target to archive preservation plus clean Git dependency discovery and the unchanged reader. That is the honest scope. Proceed to CODE.
===== 026 END audit/2026-10-13-audit-cargo-packaging/review/plan-v2/result.md =====

require 'json';require 'digest'
r='/tmp/radio-audit-packaging-pair-v3'

  when String

    raise 'unexpected ID position' unless %w[id source dependencies pkg workspace_members workspace_default_members root].include?(key)

   elsif %w[manifest_path src_path target_directory build_directory workspace_root].include?(key)

     old,new=matches.first;z=new+v.delete_prefix(old);substitutions<<{pointer:pointer,old:v,new:z,kind:'validated-path-boundary'};z
    else
     raise 'unexpected unnormalized path' unless key=='src_path' && v==r+'/frozen/reader_probe.rs'
     v
    end
   else;v
   end
  else;v
  end
 end

end

===== 039 BEGIN audit/2026-10-13-audit-cargo-packaging/pair/frozen/reader_probe.rs | 9574 bytes | SHA256 c591a5f63d2a8079ed757fbcdf4a00d9f4b281f2ffe0abff657ebf7731058b15 =====
//! Literal consumer of the committed strict reader: no hash/encoder/traversal oracle.
use std::{fs, iter::FusedIterator, ops::Range, path::Path};

use cyber_bao::{
    BlockSize,
    proof::{ProofItem, ProofVerifier, VerifiedProof},
};

#[derive(Clone, Copy)]
enum Event {
    Parent,
    Leaf {
        proof: usize,
        file: u64,
        bytes: usize,
    },
}
struct Case {
    name: &'static str,
    directory: &'static str,
    proof_file: &'static str,
    root: &'static str,
    size: u64,
    log: u8,
    request: Range<u64>,
    actions: usize,
    wire_bytes: usize,
    events: &'static [Event],
}
fn root(hex: &str) -> [u8; 32] {
    assert_eq!(hex.len(), 64);
    std::array::from_fn(|i| u8::from_str_radix(&hex[2 * i..2 * i + 2], 16).unwrap())
}
fn finish<'a>(case: &Case, proof: &'a [u8]) -> (VerifiedProof<'a>, usize) {
    let mut job = ProofVerifier::new(
        proof,
        root(case.root),
        case.size,
        BlockSize(case.log),
        case.request.clone(),
    )
    .unwrap();
    let mut calls = 0;
    for _ in 1..case.actions {
        assert!(!job.is_finished(), "{} early terminal", case.name);
        calls += 1;
        assert!(job.step().unwrap().is_none(), "{} early view", case.name);
    }
    assert!(!job.is_finished());
    calls += 1;
    let view = job
        .step()
        .unwrap()
        .expect("literal EOF action returns view");
    assert!(job.is_finished());
    for _ in 0..10 {
        assert!(job.step().unwrap().is_none());
    }
    (view, calls) // the view survives the verifier and still borrows the proof
}
fn fused<T: FusedIterator>(_: &T) {}
fn run(base: &Path, case: &Case) {
    let dir = base.join(case.directory);
    let proof = fs::read(dir.join(case.proof_file)).unwrap();
    let body = fs::read(dir.join("body.bin")).unwrap();
    let selected = fs::read(dir.join("selected.bin")).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(proof.len(), case.wire_bytes);
    assert_eq!(u64::try_from(body.len()).unwrap(), case.size);
    assert_eq!(
        selected,
        body[usize::try_from(case.request.start).unwrap()
            ..usize::try_from(case.request.end).unwrap()]
    );
    let (view, calls) = finish(case, &proof);
    assert_eq!(calls, case.actions);
    assert_eq!(view.root(), root(case.root));
    assert_eq!(view.size(), case.size);
    assert_eq!(view.block_size(), BlockSize(case.log));
    assert_eq!(view.request(), case.request);
    let mut items = view.items();
    fused(&items);
    assert_eq!(items.is_complete(), case.events.is_empty());
    let mut copied = Vec::new();
    let mut emitted = 0;
    let mut leaves = 0;
    for (index, expected) in case.events.iter().enumerate() {
        assert!(!items.is_complete());
        emitted += 1;
        match (items.next().unwrap(), expected) {
            (ProofItem::Parent, Event::Parent) => {}
            (
                ProofItem::Leaf {
                    file_offset,
                    proof_offset,
                    bytes,
                },
                Event::Leaf {
                    proof: p,
                    file: f,
                    bytes: n,
                },
            ) => {
                leaves += 1;
                assert_eq!((proof_offset, file_offset, bytes.len()), (*p, *f, *n));
                assert_eq!(bytes.as_ptr(), proof[*p..].as_ptr());
                assert_eq!(bytes, &proof[*p..*p + *n]);
                let file = usize::try_from(*f).unwrap();
                assert_eq!(bytes, &body[file..file + *n]);
                let first = case.request.start.max(*f) - *f;
                let end = case.request.end.min(*f + u64::try_from(*n).unwrap()) - *f;
                copied.extend_from_slice(
                    &bytes[usize::try_from(first).unwrap()..usize::try_from(end).unwrap()],
                );
                println!(
                    "{{\"case\":\"{}\",\"leaf\":{},\"proof_offset\":{},\"file_offset\":{},\"bytes\":{}}}",
                    case.name,
                    leaves,
                    proof_offset,
                    file_offset,
                    bytes.len()
                );
            }
            _ => panic!(
                "{} event {} disagrees with literal carrier",
                case.name, index
            ),
        }
        assert_eq!(items.is_complete(), index + 1 == case.events.len());
    }
    assert_eq!(emitted, case.events.len());
    assert!(items.is_complete());
    for _ in 0..10 {
        assert!(items.next().is_none());
    }
    assert_eq!(copied, selected);
    println!(
        "{{\"case\":\"{}\",\"root\":\"{}\",\"verification_actions\":{},\"output_items\":{},\"leaves\":{},\"selected_bytes\":{},\"status\":\"pass\"}}",
        case.name,
        case.root,
        calls,
        emitted,
        leaves,
        copied.len()
    );
}

  }
}

===== 098 BEGIN audit/2026-10-13-audit-cargo-packaging/verify.nu | 8799 bytes | SHA256 e208a0859470dd834c5986cbbb6b40585681693feacb5fb16719e3c97a1234fa =====
use std/assert

def check [root: string, f: record] {
  let b = (open --raw ($root | path join $f.path) | into binary)
  assert equal ($b | bytes length) $f.bytes
  assert equal ($b | hash sha256) $f.sha256
}
def pointer [p: string] {
  $p | split row '/' | skip 1 | each {|x| if ($x =~ '^[0-9]+$') { $x | into int } else { $x } } | into cell-path
}
def main [--repo: string, --live] {
  assert ($repo != null) 'Pass --repo containing the exact historical Radio Git objects'
  let root = $env.FILE_PWD
  let files = (open ($root | path join files.json))
  for f in $files { check $root $f }
  let base = 'ce0fb312faa8770e80efe9afa864106ae6b51552'
  let tmp = (^mktemp -d /tmp/radio-packaging-verify.XXXXXX | str trim)
  let blob = ($tmp | path join blob)
  let sources = (open ($root | path join inputs/source.json) | append (open ($root | path join inputs/v2-additional-source.json)))
  for f in $sources {
    ^git -C $repo cat-file blob $'($f.revision):($f.path)' out> $blob
    assert equal $env.LAST_EXIT_CODE 0
    let bytes = (open --raw $blob | into binary)
    assert equal ($bytes | bytes length) $f.bytes
    assert equal ($bytes | hash sha256) $f.sha256
    assert equal (open --raw ($root | path join $f.local) | into binary) $bytes
  }
  let renames = (open ($root | path join inputs/renames.json))
  assert equal ($renames | length) 7
  assert equal ($renames | get bytes | math sum) 2141
  let bindings = (open ($root | path join inputs/historical-index-bindings.json))
  for f in $bindings {
    ^git -C $repo cat-file blob $'($base):($f.index)' out> $blob
    assert equal $env.LAST_EXIT_CODE 0
    let text = (open --raw $blob)
    assert equal ($text | hash sha256) $f.index_sha256
    let entry = ($text | from json | get (pointer $f.pointer))
    assert equal $entry.bytes $f.bytes
    assert equal $entry.sha256 $f.sha256
    if $live { check $repo {path: $f.archived_path, bytes: $f.bytes, sha256: $f.sha256} }
  }
  # Bundle is an incremental local witness, not a release or default-branch commit.
  let bare = ($tmp | path join witness.git)
  ^git clone --bare --shared $repo $bare out+err> ($tmp | path join clone.log)
  assert equal $env.LAST_EXIT_CODE 0
  ^git --git-dir $bare fetch ($root | path join pair/candidate.bundle) refs/heads/packaging-witness out+err> ($tmp | path join fetch.log)
  assert equal $env.LAST_EXIT_CODE 0
  let snap = (open ($root | path join pair/snapshot.json))
  assert equal $snap.parent $base
  assert equal (^git --git-dir $bare rev-parse FETCH_HEAD | str trim) $snap.candidate
  assert equal (^git --git-dir $bare rev-parse $'($snap.candidate)^{tree}' | str trim) $snap.tree
  let delta = (^git --git-dir $bare diff-tree --no-commit-id --no-renames --name-only -r $base $snap.candidate | lines | sort)
  assert equal $delta ($snap.explicit_paths | get path | sort)
  assert equal $delta ($snap.changed_paths | sort)
  for f in ($snap.explicit_paths | where {|x| not ($x | get -o deleted | default false) }) {
    ^git --git-dir $bare cat-file blob $'($snap.candidate):($f.path)' out> $blob
    assert equal $env.LAST_EXIT_CODE 0
    let b = (open --raw $blob | into binary)
    assert equal ($b | bytes length) $f.bytes
    assert equal ($b | hash sha256) $f.sha256
  }
  let normal = {|rev| ^git --git-dir $bare ls-tree -r $rev | lines | where {|s| let p = ($s | split row "\t" | last); not (($p | str starts-with audit/) or ($p | str starts-with .claude/tasks/)) } }
  assert equal (do $normal $base) (do $normal $snap.candidate)
  assert equal (do $normal $base | length) $snap.unchanged_normal_paths
  let names = (^git --git-dir $bare ls-tree -r --name-only $snap.candidate audit | lines)
  assert ($names | where {|p| ($p | path basename) == Cargo.toml } | is-empty)
  let freeze = (open ($root | path join execution/source-freeze.json))
  for f in $freeze {
    ^git --git-dir $bare cat-file blob $'($snap.candidate):($f.path)' out> $blob
    assert equal $env.LAST_EXIT_CODE 0
    assert equal (open --raw $blob | hash sha256) $f.sha256
    if $live { check $repo $f }
  }
  # Raw stream hashes and actual statuses, including deliberate failures.
  let raw = (open ($root | path join raw-records.json))
  for f in $raw {
    let r = (open ($root | path join $f.path))
    assert equal $r.exit_code $f.exit_code
    for stream in [stdout stderr] {
      let s = ($r | get $stream)
      let b = (open --raw ($root | path join ($f.path | str replace '.json' $'.($stream)')) | into binary)
      assert equal ($b | bytes length) $s.bytes
      assert equal ($b | hash sha256) $s.sha256
    }
  }
  for arm in [parent candidate] {
    let before = (open ($root | path join pair $arm before.json))
    assert equal $before (open ($root | path join pair $arm after.json))
    for cmd in [lock metadata debug release] {
      let r = (open ($root | path join pair $arm $'($cmd).json'))
      assert equal $r.exit_code 0
      let err = (open --raw ($root | path join pair $arm $'($cmd).stderr'))
      if $arm == parent { assert ($err | str contains 'warning: skipping duplicate package') } else { assert (not ($err | str contains 'warning:')) }
    }
  }
  let result = (open ($root | path join pair/results.json))
  for arm in [parent candidate] { for mode in [debug release] {
    assert equal (open --raw ($root | path join pair $arm $'($mode).stdout') | hash sha256) $result.stdout_sha256
  } }
  assert equal $result.stdout_sha256 'ccb679634c8a07dc76b75445b6ac9f8372602e46bb4d85217a7634db6956ab99'
  let tuples = {|arm| open ($root | path join pair $arm sources.json) | select package relative bytes sha256 }
  assert equal (do $tuples parent) (do $tuples candidate)
  assert equal (do $tuples parent | length) 63
  let external = (open ($root | path join pair/external-manifest.json))
  assert equal ($external | select path bytes sha256 | uniq | length) 1
  check $root {path: pair/external.Cargo.toml.txt, bytes: ($external | first | get bytes), sha256: ($external | first | get sha256)}
  let comparison = (open ($root | path join pair/metadata-comparison.json))
  mut normalized = []
  for row in $comparison.arms {
    mut data = (open --raw ($root | path join pair $row.arm metadata.stdout) | from json)
    for s in $row.substitutions {
      let p = (pointer $s.pointer)
      assert equal ($data | get $p) $s.old
      if $s.kind == exact-identifier {
        assert equal ($row.exact_map | get $s.old) $s.new
      } else {
        assert equal $s.kind validated-path-boundary
        let admitted = ($row.path_prefix_map | transpose old new | where {|m| ($s.old == $m.old) or ($s.old | str starts-with ($m.old + '/')) })
        assert equal ($admitted | length) 1
        let m = ($admitted | first)
        assert equal $s.new ($m.new + ($s.old | str substring ($m.old | str length)..))
      }
      $data = ($data | update $p $s.new)
    }
    assert equal $data (open ($root | path join pair $row.arm metadata-normalized.json))
    $normalized = ($normalized | append [$data])
  }
  assert equal ($normalized | get 0) ($normalized | get 1)
  for name in [research proof] {
    assert equal (open --raw ($root | path join records $'($name)-before.stdout')) (open --raw ($root | path join records $'($name)-after-v2.stdout'))
    let live = (open ($root | path join records $'($name)-live-after-v2.json'))
    assert equal $live.exit_code 1
    let error = (open --raw ($root | path join records $'($name)-live-after-v2.stderr'))
    assert ($error | str contains 'Failed historical replay retained at /tmp/radio-audit-replay.')
    assert ($error | str contains $base)
    assert ($error | str contains b933829cbe273d2f31fe75ca83125f3ddd8917a2)
  }
  let controls = (open ($root | path join controls/staging-v2/results.json))
  assert equal ($controls.stages | get files_equal) [13 90 620]
  for x in $controls.failures { assert equal $x.before $x.after; assert equal $x.historical_invoked false; assert equal $x.status 1 }
  assert equal ($controls.default_cli | length) 3
  assert equal (open ($root | path join controls/child-exit-v1/result.json) | get status) 77
  if $live {
    let actual = (^git -C $repo diff --no-renames --name-only $base | lines | append (^git -C $repo ls-files --others --exclude-standard | lines) | uniq | sort)
    assert equal $actual (open ($root | path join final-paths.json) | sort)
    assert (glob ($repo | path join audit '**' Cargo.toml) | is-empty)
  }
  rm --recursive $tmp
  {status: pass, files: ($files | length), historical_git_inputs: ($sources | length), index_bindings: ($bindings | length),
   packaging_files: ($freeze | length), snapshot_paths: ($delta | length), raw_records: ($raw | length),
   source_tuples: 63, consumer_cases: 8, consumer_commands_per_arm: 4, requires_exact_git_objects: true,
   cargo_rerun: false, broader_gates_refreshed: false} | to json
}

===== 098 END audit/2026-10-13-audit-cargo-packaging/verify.nu =====
